## Project Description

Risk Curators. Structurally: 46 project-authored contract(s) across 4 chain(s); 3 ERC4626 vaults, 6 ERC20 tokens, 7 Chainlink feeds; role-gated via AccessControl; upgradeable via ERC1967/UUPS proxies; built on openzeppelin.

### Architecture

The protocol comprises 8 functional families. Its contracts share 20 common project-authored base contract(s) (eip712, nonces, ownable2step). Dominant framework: openzeppelin.

## Fork Lineage

This project reuses audited code from **TermMax** (`termmax`) in the AcreBtcDepositVault, DepositVault, HypeEthDepositVault, HypeUsdDepositVault, HypeUsdRedemptionVaultWithSwapper, LeveragedQQQonDepositVault, … subsystem.
11 audits inherited from `termmax`, scoped to that subsystem.

Total inherited audits: 11. Inherited coverage reflects forked/shared code audited by the origin project — it is not a direct audit of this project.
